## Overview

Dr. Eric D. Peterson is a cardiologist in Dallas, Texas and is affiliated with multiple hospitals in the area, including [Parkland Health-Dallas](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/tx/parkland-memorial-hospital-6740950) and [UT Southwestern Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/tx/ut-southwestern-medical-center-6740343). He received his medical degree from University of Pittsburgh School of Medicine and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Eric D. Peterson accepts Humana, Blue Cross, United Healthcare - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455#insurance).

Doctor's Details

## Specialties

Specialty

### Cardiology

Cardiologists treat diseases of the heart and blood vessels, which are collectively known as the cardiovascular system. Common conditions treated by cardiologists include genetic heart defects, heart rhythm disorders, heart failure and coronary artery disease (narrowing of the blood vessels caused by the buildup of plaque and cholesterol).

Subspecialties

## Frequently Asked Questions About Dr. Peterson

### Can I make an appointment online with Dr. Peterson?

Yes, you can [book an appointment](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455) with Dr. Peterson online today. It's simple, secure, and free.

### Does Dr. Peterson offer telehealth?

Call [Dr. Peterson](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455) for more information on telehealth services.

### What do patients say about Dr. Peterson?

We’ve published patient experience ratings for Dr. Peterson in 6 key categories based on 6 reviews. [See ratings](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455#patient-experience).

### Dr. Peterson accepts insurance

Check Dr. Peterson's [profile](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455#insurance) to see which insurance they accept.

### Where is Dr. Peterson's  office located?

Dr. Peterson's office is located at
5323 Harry Hines Blvd, Dallas, TX.
[View the map](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455#location).

### What are Dr. Peterson's  areas of care?

Cardiologists treat diseases of the heart and blood vessels, which are collectively known as the cardiovascular system. Common conditions treated by cardiologists include genetic heart defects, heart rhythm disorders, heart failure and coronary artery disease (narrowing of the blood vessels caused by the buildup of plaque and cholesterol).
See all conditions on Dr. Peterson's
[profile](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/eric-peterson-38455).
